Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The biological variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 656 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 838 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the biological decomposition. By minimality, the element a^656 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 838-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
